We evaluate our tool through an empirical study on real-world merge scenarios across multiple programming languages, comparing it with unstructured, semistructured, and structured tools. Our results show that increasing structural granularity improves automatic conflict resolution but can also lead to more aggressive merge decisions, increasing the number of missed actual conflicts. In contrast, MergirafSemi achieves a more balanced trade-off, reducing spurious conflicts while maintaining competitive accuracy and better runtime performance in most common scenarios. Compared to an unstructured tool, it substantially reduces spurious conflicts, and when compared to a semistructured language-specific tool, it achieves comparable effectiveness while exhibiting more robust execution behavior and significantly lower runtime overhead.

</details>


### [168] [The Devil Is in the Interface: Evaluating How Tool Architecture Shapes Coding Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11386)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hamidreza Saghir,Qianhui Wu,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Tong Wang,Kiran Lakkaraju,Kexin Pei,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验系统研究编程智能体中的"工具架构"（tool architecture）设计维度，比较六种在底层信息与操作相同但组织与暴露方式不同的工具架构对智能体行为的影响，基于3个actor和11,700条轨迹的实验表明：更结构化的低层接口可将重复尝试一致性提升至4.7倍，自然语言搜索可增加11%以上的相关文件访问，Python CodeAct风格接口可减少41.6%的步骤和56.3%的token消耗，而轻量级认知脚手架工具影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于智能体工具的研究主要关注扩展智能体的能力范围（能做什么），但很少系统性地关注这些能力如何被组织和暴露给模型。作者将此设计维度定义为"工具架构"（tool architecture），并主张这一维度对智能体行为有重要但被忽视的影响。

Method: 在仓库级issue修复任务上设计受控实验，比较六种工具架构，这些架构在底层信息和操作维度保持一致，仅改变工具的组织与暴露方式。实验共涉及3个actor（不同模型）和11,700条智能体轨迹。

Result: 1) 相比仅提供bash工具的基础架构，更结构化的低层接口可将跨重复尝试的一致性提升至4.7倍；2) 自然语言搜索扩大了仓库探索范围，相关文件访问增加超过11%；3) Python CodeAct风格接口以41.6%更少的步骤和56.3%更低的token消耗达到相似的任务性能；4) 轻量级基于文本的认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理）对actor行为影响有限。

Conclusion: 工具架构是一个影响智能体行为的关键设计维度，即使工具能力相似，不同的组织与暴露方式也会显著改变智能体的效率、一致性和探索行为。研究为智能体工具设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: As large language models continue to improve, agentic systems are becoming increasingly important, and tools are a key design dimension because they determine how agents access information and take action in their environments. Prior work on agent tooling has primarily focused on expanding what agents can do, but has paid less systematic attention to how those capabilities are organized and exposed to the model. We refer to this latter design dimension as tool architecture. We study tool architecture in coding agents through controlled experiments on repository-level issue fixing, comparing six tool architectures that hold the underlying information and actions similar while varying how they are organized and exposed to the model, across three actors and a total of 11,700 trajectories. Our experiments show that, even when tools provide similar capabilities, tool architecture changes agent behavior: Compared to a basic architecture where the agent has only the bash tool, more structured low-level interfaces improve consistency across repeated attempts by up to 4.7 $\times$; natural-language search broadens repository exploration and increases access to relevant files by more than 11%; and Python CodeAct-style interfaces achieve similar task performance with 41.6% fewer steps and 56.3% lower token usage. By contrast, lightweight text-based cognitive-scaffolding tools, such as tools that let the agent record intermediate reasoning, have limited effect on actor behavior.

</details>


### [169] [GraphAlignCoder: Aligning Program and Proof Graphs for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11394)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphAlignCoder，一种通过构建实现图和形式化证明流图来将显式正确性结构迁移到代码生成的训练框架，在多个基准上大幅超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码大语言模型能生成语法合理的程序，但常违反隐藏的语义约束。已有的执行反馈训练方法只能判断程序是否失败，无法提供关于正确解决方案应如何组织的细粒度监督信息。

Method: GraphAlignCoder包含两个并行流程：(1)构建实现图，捕获程序区域间的控制流和数据依赖关系；(2)通过受限的Lean流水线产生证明轨迹，并从中提取形式化证明流图。模型首先学习可执行代码以及基于图描述的各程序区域正确性原因，然后将这些知识整合到代码生成中。

Result: GraphAlignCoder在所有基准上一致优于基础模型、仅代码SFT和CodeRL。相比CodeRL，在LiveCodeBench v6上解决数量从38提升到50（+31.6%），在BigCodeBench Hard上从16提升到23（+43.8%），在BigCodeBench Full上从359提升到363。

Conclusion: 消融研究表明，验证图注入带来初始推理能力提升，而验证到代码的整合对跨基准的鲁棒迁移至关重要。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically plausible programs that nevertheless violate hidden semantic constraints. Existing execution-feedback training methods identify whether a completed program fails, but provide limited supervision about how a correct solution should be organized. We introduce GraphAlignCoder, a training framework that transfers explicit correctness structure into code generation.
  GraphAlignCoder constructs an implementation graph that captures control and dependence among program regions. In parallel, a constrained Lean pipeline produces proof traces, from which we extract a formal proof-flow graph. The model first learns executable code together with graph-derived descriptions of why individual program regions are correct, and then consolidates this knowledge into code generation. GraphAlignCoder consistently outperforms the base model, code-only SFT, and CodeRL across all benchmarks. Compared with CodeRL, it increases the solved count from 38 to 50 on LiveCodeBench v6 and from 16 to 23 on BigCodeBench Hard, corresponding to relative gains of 31.6% and 43.8%, while also improving BigCodeBench Full from 359 to 363 tasks. The ablation study further shows that verification-graph injection produces the initial reasoning gain, while verification to code consolidation is essential for robust cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [170] [Simplifying Requirements Engineering in the Context of the LGPD: An LLM-Based Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11454)
*Cinara Gomes de Melo Carneiro,Renato de Freitas Bulcão Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在巴西通用数据保护法（LGPD）框架下简化需求工程（RE）的可行性，通过自动生成用户故事和验收测试场景来实现隐私法规的合规性，并取得了高性能的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规的合规性对需求工程构成复杂挑战，因为需要将法律规范转化为软件需求。传统方法耗时且容易出错，需要探索更高效的自动化手段。

Method: 利用当前立法（LGPD）作为输入，通过大型语言模型自动生成用户故事（User Stories）和验收测试场景（Acceptance Test Scenarios）。

Result: 评估结果显示该方法表现出高性能，验证了LLMs在确保软件初期监管合规性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有效简化隐私法规下的需求工程流程，有望从软件开发的初始阶段就保障法规合规性。

Abstract: Compliance with privacy legislation poses a complex challenge to Requirements Engineering (RE): translating legal norms into software requirements. In this context, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can simplify RE within the framework of the Brazilian General Data Protection Law (LGPD). The proposed approach utilizes current legislation to automatically generate User Stories and Acceptance Test Scenarios. The evaluation results demonstrated high performance, confirming the potential of LLMs to ensure regulatory compliance from the software's inception.

</details>


### [171] [Do Influence Tactics Matter? Investigating Prompt Framing Effects in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11513)
*Alex Deaconu,Anubhav Gupta,Manaal Basha,Nicholas Haydu,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究了基于心理学影响力策略（Yukl & Falbe的八种影响战术）的提示词框架对LLM代码生成任务的影响，发现某些策略（如强调紧迫性）会降低代码的正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词措辞和结构已知会影响LLM性能，但心理学启发下的提示词框架影响尚未被探索。人类在说服和激励他人时会使用多种沟通策略，这些策略是否也能影响LLM在编码任务中的行为是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 将Yukl & Falbe的八种影响战术（如理性说服、讨好、交换等）操作化为可复现的提示词模板，在五个开源LLM上使用LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified两个基准进行评估，从功能正确性、质量、可维护性和安全性四个维度衡量代码输出。

Result: 某些影响力诱导的提示词框架（特别是强调紧迫性的策略）与正确性和安全性的降低相关。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于语言线索如何塑造LLM输出的洞见，并为设计透明、可解释的人机交互代码生成提出实用建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows, helping developers write, debug, test, and maintain code. While prompt wording and structure are known to influence model performance, the impact of psychologically inspired prompt framings remains unexplored. This study investigates whether different psychology-based communication strategies that humans use to persuade or motivate others can lead to more effective prompt framing, which may, in turn, affect LLM behaviour in coding tasks. Drawing on Yukl & Falbe's well-known taxonomy, we operationalized eight influence tactics (like rational persuasion, ingratiation, and exchange) into reproducible prompt templates. These prompt templates were evaluated across five leading open-weight LLMs using two widely adopted benchmarks: LiveCodeBench and SWE-bench Verified. We assessed the resulting code output on four key software quality dimensions: functional correctness, quality, maintainability, and security. Our results show that certain influence-induced prompt framings, particularly those emphasizing urgency, were associated with reduced correctness and security. This work presents the first large-scale empirical study of influence-induced prompt framing in software engineering tasks, offering insights into how linguistic cues may shape LLM outputs. We conclude with practical insights for designing transparent and interpretable human-AI interactions in code generation.

</details>


### [172] [Harnessing LLMs for Document-Guided Fuzzing of Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11744)
*Bin Duan,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Yan Yan,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VistaFuzz是一种文档引导的模糊测试技术，利用本地部署的开源LLM从API文档中提取参数规范，生成同时满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入，用于测试Python库的API可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库在深度学习、科学计算等领域至关重要，其API可靠性直接影响下游应用。现有测试方法要么将约束隐含在生成的程序中，要么依赖特定库的解析规则，无法有效处理参数间的依赖关系。

Method: VistaFuzz使用本地托管的开源LLM从API文档中提取参数规范，并生成满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入。该方法通过文档引导的方式，自动理解和解析API文档中的参数约束信息。

Result: 在12个Python库的7,718个API上评估表明：40.1%的API存在参数间关系；禁用依赖关系解析后，有效生成率从95%以上降至31.6%-52.8%。VistaFuzz报告了74个问题，其中43个已获开发者确认，29个已修复。

Conclusion: VistaFuzz通过文档引导和LLM技术有效解决了Python API测试中输入约束和参数依赖的难题，显著提高了有效输入生成率，并成功发现了大量真实问题。

Abstract: Python libraries underpin deep learning, scientific computing, data analysis, and computer vision, making their reliability critical to downstream applications. Testing their APIs requires inputs that satisfy both per-parameter constraints and dependencies among parameters. Existing approaches either leave such constraints implicit in generated programs or rely on library-specific parsing rules. This paper introduces VistaFuzz, a document-guided fuzzing technique that uses a locally served open-sourced LLM to extract parameter specifications from API documents and generate inputs that satisfy both parameter constraints and inter-parameter dependencies. We evaluate VistaFuzz on 7,718 APIs across twelve Python libraries. Inter-parameter relationships occur in 40.1\% of tested APIs, and disabling their resolution reduces the valid generation rate on those APIs from above 95\% to 31.6\%--52.8\%. VistaFuzz reports 74 issues, of which 43 have been confirmed by developers and 29 have been fixed.

</details>


### [173] [Instruction Alignment for Binary Code Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11766)
*Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在二进制代码表示学习中显式引入指令级对齐作为辅助训练目标，以弥补现有方法只关注函数级嵌入而忽略细粒度指令对应关系的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制代码表示学习方法主要学习函数级嵌入，捕捉粗粒度的语义关系，忽略了细粒度的指令级对应关系。这一局限性浪费了编译器调试信息中可用的宝贵监督信号，而这些信号能够支持学习更准确和可解释的二进制代码表示。

Method: 研究发现经过函数级二进制代码相似性微调的模型比预训练模型展现出更好的指令对齐能力，表明指令对齐与函数级嵌入质量之间存在强相关性。基于此观察，作者设计了一种训练方法，将指令对齐显式纳入为辅助训练目标。

Result: 实验表明，指令对齐训练提高了检索准确率，并为模型的相似性判断提供了更具区分度的信号。

Conclusion: 指令对齐作为辅助训练目标能够有效提升二进制代码表示学习的质量，使模型不仅更准确也更可解释。

Abstract: Binary code representation learning is a fundamental problem in software security and reverse engineering. Existing methods mainly learn function-level embeddings that capture coarse-grained semantic relationships between binary functions, but they largely ignore fine-grained instruction-level correspondences. This limitation misses valuable supervision signals available from compiler debug information, which can support the learning of more accurate and interpretable binary code representations.
  We propose to leverage instruction alignment knowledge to further improve binary code representation learning. Our preliminary study reveals that models finetuned for function-level binary code similarity exhibit substantially better instruction alignment than their pre-trained model, suggesting a strong correlation between instruction alignment and function-level embedding quality. Motivated by this observation, we design a training approach that explicitly incorporates instruction alignment as an auxiliary training objective. Our experiments show that instruction alignment training improves retrieval accuracy and provides more discriminative signal for the model's similarity judgments.

</details>


### [174] [Testing Deep Learning Library APIs via Cross-Framework Differential Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11886)
*Bin Duan,Ruican Dong,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Xamt是一种跨框架差分模糊测试方法，用于深度学习库API测试。它通过构造跨七个库的执行验证API等价组，使用方差引导的差分模糊测试，发现72个独立复现的差异案例，其中25个已被开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习库的API级测试技术依赖库内属性或CPU-GPU差分预言，可能遗漏跨硬件后端行为一致的缺陷。需要跨框架的差分测试方法来发现这些缺陷。

Method: Xamt通过三个步骤实现：(1) 使用显式API别名和参数角色规范化构造候选对应关系，通过成对执行和组级行为检查验证执行有效性；(2) 使用方差引导的差分模糊测试，覆盖普通、边界和非有限输入；(3) 使用崩溃和不一致性预言标记异常终止或输出不一致的情况进行复现和分析。

Result: 在七个库中构建了676个执行验证组，包含2563个匹配API。识别出72个独立复现的差异案例（4个崩溃，68个输出不一致），其中25个已被开发者确认，23个已修复。

Conclusion: Xamt证明了跨框架差分模糊测试在深度学习库API测试中的有效性，能够发现被现有技术遗漏的跨库一致性缺陷。

Abstract: Deep learning libraries underpin many safety- and reliability-critical applications, yet existing API-level testing techniques often rely on intra-library properties or CPU--GPU differential oracles and may miss defects that behave consistently across hardware backends. We present Xamt, a cross-framework differential fuzzing approach for deep learning library APIs. Xamt constructs and tests execution-validated groups of APIs intended to implement equivalent operations across seven libraries. It uses explicit API aliases and parameter-role normalization to construct candidate correspondences and validates them through pairwise execution and a group-level behavioral check on canonical ordinary inputs. The resulting groups are explored using variance-guided differential fuzzing with ordinary, boundary, and non-finite inputs. Crash and inconsistency oracles flag executions exhibiting abnormal termination or inconsistent outputs for subsequent reproduction and analysis. Across the seven libraries, Xamt constructs 676 execution-validated groups containing 2,563 matched APIs. Among these, Xamt identifies 72 independently reproduced discrepancy cases, including 4 crash cases and 68 output inconsistencies. Among the 72 developer reports, 25 have been confirmed, including 23 that have been fixed.

</details>


### [175] [Developing LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering: A Mixed-Method Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11965)
*Mariama Celi Serafim De Oliveira,Motunrayo Osatohanmen Ibiyo,Marco Gianrusso,Claudio Di Sipio,Davide Di Ruscio,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程中用于实现多智能体系统（MAS）的现有工具和框架进行了全面综述。作者对最相关的开源MAS框架进行了定量分析（评估文档、特性和能力），并对部分框架进行了定性评估（通过实现README.MD文件摘要用例）。研究发现所选框架对MAS的基本组件覆盖良好，但缺少高级功能如智能体遥测；在摘要任务上各框架ROUGE得分无显著差异。最后提供了经验教训和挑战，帮助研究者和实践者根据需求选择合适的MAS框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）正在改变软件开发过程，但开发者在选择合适技术、设计协调规则和智能体角色时面临诸多挑战。目前缺乏对现有MAS工具和框架的系统性评估，导致开发者难以做出明智选择。

Method: 采用混合方法：（1）对最相关的开源MAS框架进行定量分析，从开发者视角评估其文档、特性和能力；（2）对部分选定框架进行定性评估，实现一个共同用例——README.MD文件摘要，并使用ROUGE分数进行对比。

Result: 所选框架对MAS的基本组件覆盖良好，但高级功能（如智能体遥测）仍然缺失。在README.MD摘要任务中，各框架之间的ROUGE得分没有显著差异。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一套经验教训和挑战，可帮助研究者和实践者根据自身需求选择合适的MAS框架。同时指出了当前框架在高级特性方面的不足，为未来的框架发展提供了方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of Generative Artificial Intelligence (Gen AI) powered by large language models (LLMs) has transformed the software development process, introducing new paradigms for code generation, debugging, testing, and maintenance. While early applications focused on leveraging single, independent LLMs to assist developers with isolated tasks, recent advances have shifted toward multi-agent systems (MAS) that orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents working collaboratively toward common objectives. Despite their promising potential, using MAS encompasses a set of challenges for developers who have to carefully select the right technology, devise proper coordination rules, and design specific roles for the involved agents. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive overview of the existing tools and frameworks for implementing MAS in software engineering. First, we conducted a quantitative analysis of the most relevant open source MAS frameworks by evaluating their documentation, features, and capabilities from the developers' perspective. Second, we performed a qualitative evaluation of a subset of the selected frameworks by implementing a common use case: the summarization of README.MD files. The findings show that the selected frameworks provide a good coverage of fundamental components of MAS, though advanced features such as telemetry of agents are still missing. In addition, the empirical evaluation shows that there is no significant difference in terms of ROUGE scores considering the summarization task. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned and challenges that can help researchers and practitioners to select a suitable MAS framework according to their needs.

</details>


### [176] [RealisticTritonBench: A Benchmark for Triton-Kernel Generation in Real-World AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12004)
*Jinjun Huang,Zhongzhen Wen,Tongtong Xu,Meng Yan,Xin Xia,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了RealisticTritonBench，这是首个基于真实AI框架Pull Request构建的Triton内核生成基准，通过端到端测试评估LLM生成的内核，解决了现有基准在任务多样性、评估标准和正确性检查方面的三大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成Triton内核的基准存在三个关键局限：(1)仅限制于PyTorch到Triton的翻译任务，无法反映真实世界Triton任务的多样性和复杂性；(2)只评估单个内核性能而非端到端性能，而后者才是实际部署的核心标准；(3)依赖手工编写的评估脚本，可能存在模型可利用的漏洞来规避正确性检查并获得虚高分数。

Method: 从流行的开源AI框架中系统性地提取修改Triton内核的Pull Requests，将其转化为带有具体工程上下文的生成任务。每个任务以自然语言需求为输入，要求生成对应的Triton内核实现，并提供完整可复现的评估环境。与以往只关注孤立内核性能的基准不同，RealisticTritonBench将生成的内核集成回原始框架，使用端到端测试进行评估。

Result: 在RealisticTritonBench上评估了领先的LLM，发现它们在真实世界的Triton内核生成任务上仍然面临困难。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在真实世界Triton内核生成方面仍存在显著不足，RealisticTritonBench为更真实、更严格的评估提供了新标准，凸显了该领域仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: In modern AI frameworks, GPU kernels are key to overall system performance. Combining usability, portability, and near-handwritten CUDA performance, Triton is widely adopted for implementing GPU kernels. Recent advances show the potential of large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate Triton kernels, reducing the manual effort required from expert kernel developers. Several benchmarks evaluate LLM-generated Triton kernels. However, they suffer from three key limitations: (1) they restrict tasks to PyTorch-to-Triton translation, failing to reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world Triton tasks; (2) they evaluate only individual-kernel performance rather than end-to-end performance, the core criterion for real-world deployment in AI frameworks; and (3) they rely on manually written evaluation scripts for individual kernels, which may contain flaws that models can exploit to bypass correctness checks and obtain inflated scores. To address these limitations, we introduce RealisticTritonBench, the first benchmark to derive Triton kernel generation tasks from real-world pull requests in popular AI frameworks, enabling realistic, production-like evaluation. RealisticTritonBench systematically extracts PRs that modify Triton kernels from popular open-source AI frameworks and transforms them into generation tasks with concrete engineering contexts. Each task takes a natural language requirement as input and requires a corresponding Triton kernel implementation, with a complete and reproducible evaluation environment. Unlike prior benchmarks focused on isolated kernel performance, RealisticTritonBench integrates generated kernels into their original frameworks and evaluates them using end-to-end tests, enabling a more faithful assessment. We evaluate leading LLMs on RealisticTritonBench and find that they still struggle with real-world Triton kernel generation tasks.

</details>


### [177] [From Safety Documentation to Safety Knowledge Support: An Evidence-Grounded LLM Framework for Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12025)
*Tuhinangshu Gangopadhyay,Rasmus Adler,Peter Liggesmeyer,Jan Reich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）为医疗器械安全工程提供源链接的安全知识支持，而非单纯的安全文本生成。框架连接设备工件、受控知识存储与检索、方法特定的候选安全项生成、批判与不确定性检查以及记录在案的专家评审，并提出了相应的评估策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械开发需要符合ISO 14971和IEC 62304的风险管理证据，且证据需在需求、设计、软件变更、验证结果、投诉和上市后数据之间保持一致。这些任务成本高昂且依赖稀缺的安全与领域专家。现有LLM安全工程研究多聚焦孤立方法、依赖通用提示或公开示例，在源链接、可追溯性、不确定性处理、生命周期更新和专家评审记录方面支持有限，限制了其在受监管医疗器械开发中的应用。

Method: 提出一个证据驱动的框架，包含四个关键部分：(1) 连接设备工件；(2) 受控知识存储与检索；(3) 方法特定的候选安全项生成；(4) 批判与不确定性检查以及记录在案的专家评审。框架负责准备、链接、检查和更新候选安全工件，供专家决策使用，但不决定设备是否安全，也不提供监管批准。

Result: 论文提出了一种评估策略，使用非公开或新建的医疗器械案例研究和专家参考分析，从覆盖率、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无依据声明和评审工作量等维度进行评估。

Conclusion: 核心研究问题不是安全文本生成，而是源链接的安全知识支持。该框架旨在减少安全工程师的重复性工作，同时保持人类专家在最终决策中的核心地位，适用于受监管的医疗器械开发环境。

Abstract: Medical devices are becoming more software-intensive, connected, and AI-enabled. Their development requires risk-management evidence aligned with ISO 14971 and, for software, IEC 62304. This evidence must be kept consistent across requirements, design decisions, software changes, verification results, complaints, and post-market data. These tasks are costly and depend on scarce safety and domain experts.
  Large language models (LLMs) may reduce parts of this effort because medical-device safety work is highly document-based. However, current LLM-based safety-engineering studies often address isolated methods, rely on generic prompting or public examples, and provide limited support for source links, traceability, uncertainty handling, lifecycle updates, and recorded expert review. This limits their use in regulated medical-device development.
  This paper argues that the central research problem is not safety-text generation, but source-linked safety-knowledge support. We propose an evidence-grounded framework that connects device artifacts, controlled knowledge storage and retrieval, method-specific generation of candidate safety items, critique and uncertainty checks, and recorded expert review. The framework prepares, links, checks, and updates candidate safety artifacts for expert decision-making. It does not decide whether a device is safe and does not provide regulatory approval. We also outline an evaluation strategy using non-public or newly built medical-device case studies and expert reference analyses to assess coverage, correctness, relevance, traceability, duplicate rate, unsupported claims, and review effort.

</details>


### [178] [ADEPT: A Unified Framework for Deep Learning Test Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12144)
*Yidi Kao,Shawn Burnham,Tommi Rose Fahy,Ali Ghanbari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个集成多种深度学习测试充分性度量指标的框架，通过统一的执行流程、模板化接口和YAML配置管理，解决了研究原型难以复现、比较和采用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年提出了许多深度学习测试充分性度量指标（如神经元覆盖率、潜在特征覆盖、决策边界探索等），但它们通常以独立研究原型发布，具有不同的安装要求、预处理流程、执行工作流和配置机制，导致难以复现、比较和在实际部署中采用。

Method: ADEPT在一致的执行工作流下集成了代表性充分性技术，包括神经元覆盖率指标、意外充分性、输入分布覆盖、边界覆盖以及源级和模型级变异分数。它提供基于模板的度量接口和明确定义的扩展点，便于集成新指标；同时提供YAML配置管理、预处理缓存复用和结构化结果报告。

Result: ADEPT使得研究人员和从业者无需花费数天或数周时间实现缺失工具或配置不同的研究原型，即可复现和应用充分性度量指标。论文提供了演示视频链接。

Conclusion: ADEPT旨在降低深度学习测试充分性指标的使用门槛，方便在研究与开发工作流中应用这些指标，促进可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Over the past decade, many test adequacy metrics have been proposed for deep learning that characterize test dataset adequacy from different perspectives, e.g., neuron activation behavior, latent feature coverage, decision-boundary exploration, etc. However, these metrics are typically released as independent research prototypes with substantially different installation and preprocessing requirements, execution workflows, and configuration mechanisms. These complications make them quite difficult to reproduce, compare, and adopt in research work and practical deployment alike. In this paper, we present the engineering details of ADEPT, a framework that integrates representative adequacy techniques, including neuron-coverage-based metrics, surprise adequacy, input distribution coverage, boundary coverage, and source- and model-level mutation score, under a consistent execution workflow. ADEPT provides a template-based metric interface with well-defined extension points for integrating new adequacy metrics. Furthermore, it provides YAML-based configuration management, preprocessing-cache reuse, and structured result reporting, making it easy to use in any research and development workflows. ADEPT is designed for researchers and practitioners who wish to reproduce and apply adequacy metrics without spending days or weeks implementing missing tooling or configuring disparate research prototypes. A demo video is available at https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video.

</details>


### [179] [Towards Automated Domain Model Extraction from Source Code using Heuristics and Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12228)
*Alessandra Mancas,Mounir Ammam,Hyacinth Ali,Kevin Delcourt,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用轻量级本地可部署LLM从源代码中自动提取领域模型的方法，通过结合结构和语义启发式与迭代式LLM推理来克服上下文窗口限制，在不需全系统上下文的情况下识别领域概念并细化领域边界，在十个项目数据集上取得高F1分数，适用于隐私敏感的工业环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大型语言模型因隐私和保密约束无法用于许多工业场景，而可本地运行的紧凑开源LLM受限于上下文窗口，无法直接处理大型代码库。因此需要一种能利用轻量级本地LLM从源代码中提取领域模型的自动化方法。

Method: 该方法结合结构性启发式和语义性启发式，并采用迭代式LLM推理机制。通过逐步分析排序后的代码元素子集来克服上下文限制，渐进式地识别领域概念并细化领域边界，无需依赖完整的系统上下文。

Result: 在包含十个项目的数据集（每个项目包含人工整理的领域模型及其对应实现）上取得了高F1分数，且方法完全可在本地部署的LLM上执行。

Conclusion: 该方法的完全本地化执行特性使其特别适合隐私敏感工业环境中的逆向工程任务，为在受限环境中利用LLM进行领域建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities for code understanding, making them promising for reverse engineering domain models from source code. However, state-ofthe- art proprietary LLMs cannot be used in many industrial contexts due to privacy and confidentiality constraints, while compact open-source LLMs that can run locally are limited by their context window and cannot process large code bases directly. In this paper, we propose an automated approach to extract domain models from source code using lightweight, locally deployable LLMs. Our method combines structural and semantic heuristics with iterative LLM-based reasoning to overcome context limitations. By progressively analyzing ranked subsets of code elements, the approach identifies domain concepts and refines domain boundaries without requiring full-system context. Our approach achieves high F1-scores on a dataset of ten projects, each comprising a curated domain model and its corresponding implementation, while remaining fully executable on locally deployable LLMs. This makes it particularly suitable for reverse engineering tasks in privacy-sensitive industrial environments.

</details>


### [180] [The Role Specialization Model (RSM): Coordinating LLM-Based Tools in Agentic Software Development - An Exploratory Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12311)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出角色专业化模型（RSM），通过探索性案例研究协调三种基于LLM的工具（Antigravity、Gemini CLI、Qwen Code）在Agentic软件工程（SE 3.0）范式下协同完成软件开发工作流，并对最终产品依据ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已从单一工具发展到可自主管理整个开发生命周期的Agentic Software Engineering（SE 3.0）范式。然而，如何协调不同能力、不同后端的多个LLM工具在真实开发工作流中协同工作，以及这种协作对软件产品质量的实际影响，仍缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 本文采用探索性案例研究方法。首先提出角色专业化模型（RSM）作为角色分配框架，然后选取三个各具特色的LLM工具（基于Gemini 2.5的Antigravity IDE、Gemini CLI、通过Ollama本地运行的Qwen Code），依据RSM在一个真实开发流程中分配角色，以增量方式开发一个用于交互式气候数据可视化的Python桌面应用，并记录工作流程、偏差、提示词加固技术和定性质量评估。

Result: 研究结果表明，明确的角色协调（RSM）能够支持开发周期组织和架构质量的提升，但实际执行需要审慎的协调策略、有效的上下文管理以及人工对Agent生成输出的验证。此外，在RSM执行过程中出现了与计划角色分配的偏差，这些偏差主要由工具能力差异和上下文限制等因素导致。

Conclusion: 显式的角色协调框架在Agentic软件工程中具有可行性，能够支撑开发流程的组织与架构质量的保障，但该范式并非全自动，仍需人为介入进行协调、上下文管理和输出验证，才能确保最终产品质量。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into software development workflows has given rise to a paradigm known as Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0), in which autonomous agents manage full development life cycles under human supervision. This paper presents an exploratory case study in which three LLM-based tools, Antigravity (an agentic IDE with a Gemini 2.5 backend), Gemini CLI, and Qwen Code (local execution via Ollama), are coordinated according to a role-distribution framework proposed in this work as the Role Specialization Model (RSM). Three research questions guide the study: (RQ1) how can LLM-based tools with distinct capabilities be coordinated through the RSM in a real development workflow; (RQ2) what deviations from the planned role distribution emerge during RSM execution and what factors explain them; and (RQ3) how does the resulting product compare against the ISO/IEC 25010 quality model. The objective was to propose the RSM with the incremental development of a Python desktop application for interactive climate-data visualization. The workflow, observed deviations, prompt-hardening techniques, and a qualitative quality assessment are documented. Results suggest that explicit role coordination can support development cycle organization and architectural quality, but requires deliberate coordination strategies, context management, and human verification of agent-generated outputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FarSky，一种基于生成式框架的太阳能辐照度预测方法。它通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的任务感知共享潜在表示，再利用潜在扩散模型生成未来潜在状态并解码为辐照度预测，实现概率性预测。在西班牙Almería的多年度ASI数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测性能上均优于现有方法，预测技能提升最高达11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏电力可靠并网至关重要。现有深度学习方法虽然改进了预测精度，但常受限于确定性预测，且对斜坡事件（辐照度骤变）的预测能力不足，影响电网稳定性。

Method: FarSky框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 多任务自编码器，同时学习图像重建和辐照度估计的共享潜在表示；(2) 潜在扩散模型，基于近期观测条件生成未来潜在状态，并通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测，最后直接解码为辐照度预测。

Result: 在来自西班牙Plataforma Solar de Almería的多年度ASI数据集上，FarSky在两组独立测试集上取得最佳的确定性和概率性预测性能，预测技能最高提升11个百分点，斜坡事件检测的F1分数超过60%，显著优于持久性模型、最先进的端到端方法和生成式预测方法。

Conclusion: FarSky证明了将生成模型与任务感知的潜在空间耦合相结合在太阳辐照度预测中的潜力，特别在概率预测和斜坡事件检测方面展现出明显优势，为太阳能预测领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [182] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验（2013-2015 vs 2023-2025年间的英文摘要）量化了商用AI检测器在学术诚信判断上的政策失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构使用商用AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与完全由LLM撰写的草稿，可能将两者均视为不当行为，需要通过实证研究量化这一政策失败。

Method: 对四个领域已发表的英文摘要进行受控研究：在tau=0.50的代理人工/AI标签下，测试轻量级"仅润色摘要"编辑（代表合规AI辅助）和不修改原文在不同年份区间的检测率，并分析检测分数与单词长度及学术词汇密度的关系，同时测试Undetectable AI人文化处理后的逃逸率。

Result: 合规的轻量级AI润色被标记率为64-80%（Pangram/GPTZero）；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率9-15%，其中非STEM领域远高于STEM（p<0.001）；检测分数与长词和学术词汇密度相关而非作者意图；人文化处理后检测率低于4%，FNR>96%。

Conclusion: 诚实的AI编辑比借助人文化工具的逃避行为面临更高的制裁风险，因此检测器分数不应作为学术不端行为的独立证据。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [183] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin 是一个面向 Rust 编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来定义和求解最小化问题，包含丰富的求解器目录，并对约束条件提供了一流支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基础任务（如模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练、工程参数选择），但需要一个一致且易用的方式来陈述和求解这类问题，尤其是在 Rust 生态中。

Method: Basin 作为一个 Rust 数值优化库，提供统一的接口让用户以一致的方式陈述和求解优化问题，并配备广泛的求解器目录，同时对约束条件提供一流的原生支持。

Result: 该库提供了单一路径来陈述和求解优化问题，包含多种求解器，并支持约束优化。

Conclusion: Basin 填补了 Rust 生态中数值优化库的空白，为科学计算和工程应用提供了一个一致、全面的优化求解方案。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [184] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为CQD-ERL的情境化质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器，用于热带水冷冷水机组及其空气侧的监督控制。该控制器不收敛于单一标量化策略，而是维护一个产品档案库，其中包含由数据驱动运行情境（每日天气和负荷模式聚类）与情境不变行为描述符联合索引的专门策略，通过无梯度进化算子与软演员-评论家策略梯度算子（共享一个回放缓冲区）填充。每次动作在执行前都经过确定性安全屏蔽过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近温度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并针对ASHRAE指南36基线进行全年回测评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法倾向于收敛到单一标量化策略，难以适应暖通空调系统复杂多变的气象与负荷情境。质量-多样性算法能够维持多种行为策略，但缺乏情境感知能力，无法在动态运行条件下有效决策。因此需要一种结合情境上下文与行为多样性的控制方法。

Method: CQD-ERL采用产品档案结构，由两个维度联合索引：一是数据驱动的运行情境（基于每日天气和负荷模式聚类），二是情境不变的行为描述符。档案通过两种算子共同填充：无梯度进化算子与软演员-评论家策略梯度算子，二者共享同一回放缓冲区。执行层面配置确定性安全屏蔽，确保所有动作在实施前满足安全约束。训练采用两层降阶环境模型，涵盖潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近温度和湿度约束。

Result: 控制器在模拟的Singapore商业建筑全年回测中表现良好，与ASHRAE Guideline 36行业基线相比具有竞争力，验证了情境化质量-多样性框架在暖通空调监督控制中的有效性。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL证明了将质量-多样性进化思想与情境上下文相结合，能够为热带地区水冷式冷水机组系统提供优于或媲美行业标准的自适应监督控制策略，兼顾策略多样性与安全约束。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [185] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC机床刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过将刀具轨迹数据分布到模拟客户端来模拟联邦学习场景，实验表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习，且显著优于本地客户端模型，能够支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNC加工中刀具磨损预测对产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要。机器学习方法虽有潜力，但工业环境中数据分布在不同机器、站点或组织之间，且存在数据共享限制，阻碍了集中式模型训练。因此需要一种能在不共享原始数据的情况下实现协作训练的方法。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布到多个模拟客户端以构建联邦学习场景，在客户端本地训练模型并通过联邦聚合更新全局模型，同时设置集中式学习模型和本地客户端模型作为对比基线，评估联邦学习的性能。

Result: 联邦学习性能接近集中式学习，且相比本地客户端模型有显著提升，证明了联邦学习在分布式刀具磨损预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在保护数据隐私的前提下实现分布式CNC制造环境中协作式的刀具磨损预测，为工业场景中受限数据共享条件下的机器学习应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [186] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"决策-资源"视角下的终端对称性利用方法（运输-精炼-验证三条分解），并实例化为 \method{} 方法，通过固定的运输过程结构、状态限制的过程秩、状态相关的残差秩以及序数秩的 meet 操作，为定向顺序构建任务提供完成保证，并在 CAD 装配、小程序和精确填充等场景中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构建任务在完成时具有精确的对称性，但执行过程本身是定向的、且依赖于历史。作者希望将这种"终端对称性"从理论概念转化为可复用的决策资源，用于指导有向构建过程的高效与可靠完成。

Method: 提出 transport–refine–certify 分解框架：过程证据提供方向性，终端对应关系跨等价结果运输结构，已实现状态证据在转移后精炼当前决策相关性，固定验证器认证执行。\method{} 具体实例化为：episode 固定的运输过程结构、状态受限过程秩、状态相关残差秩（在接受的转移后刷新）、以及序数秩 meet（其 top-k 集恰为两个 proposal 前缀的并集）。

Result: meet 操作在前缀覆盖下提供完成保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到最坏情况验证器查询次数的紧界；双状态构造预测了严格的后转移动态-静态分离。在 CAD 装配、小程序、精确填充中，statewise refresh 使 anytime AUC 分别提升最多 6.77、21.75、8.68 个点。在 GRN OOD 场景的 1,135 个 target-removal 回合中，\method{} 在三种规模下均取得最低的平均验证器成本上限，且状态信号可跨聚合与调度组织结构迁移。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可以被结构化地建模为有向构建中的可复用决策资源，通过运输-精炼-认证分解与 meet 机制，在理论与实证层面均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [187] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Forma模型，用于联合预测公司完整财务报表（78个科目，1-20个季度），并发布ProForma-20Q基准数据集。Forma将报表视为(科目,季度,数值)元组集合，通过掩码元组高斯似然训练，在远视距预测上超越所有基线模型（包括经典机器学习、梯度提升、时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型），其优势随预测周期延长而扩大，且预测区间覆盖良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估值中绝大部分企业价值取决于一年之后的现金流预测，但现有研究从未联合预测过一年以上的完整财务报表。此空白使得长期估值缺乏可靠的财务预测基础，需要新的方法填补。

Method: 提出ProForma-20Q基准（78个报表科目、1-20季度前瞻预测、变更空间R²评分）；设计Forma模型——一种Transformer架构，将财务报表表示为(账户,季度,数值)元组集合，以掩码元组高斯似然为目标进行训练。

Result: Forma在所有竞争方法中表现最佳，领先幅度随预测周期加长而扩大；高斯预测区间从未欠覆盖；预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且可在无显著精度损失下恢复完全一致性；元组接口支持无需重训练的情景分析，锁定未来收入路径能显著提升其余报表科目的预测精度。

Conclusion: 针对长期财务预测这一估值关键但此前未被解决的空白，专业化训练（Forma）优于通用扩展（通用大模型、时序基础模型等）。该工作为财务报表长期预测提供了可复现的基准与高效解决方案，并支持灵活的场景分析。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [188] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的解码时训练方法，通过dropout引起的交叉协方差估计跨前缀传输算子，将作者的监督残差传输到当前提示，在Logit空间近似SFT的分布效果，在三个LaMP个性化基准上达到与SFT相当的性能，且仅需不到7%的有效计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是使LLM适应目标分布的标准方法，但在个性化等场景中，每位作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重新训练，成本过高。因此需要一种无需修改权重的轻量级替代方案。

Method: WFT在作者训练序列上计算监督残差，通过从dropout诱导的交叉协方差中估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子捕捉一个上下文中的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间的修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得跨数据集最佳平均性能，在单个任务上匹配或超越SFT，并平均优于其他轻量级基线。在受控预算比较中，WFT仅用不到7%的有效计算量即接近SFT性能。Logit级分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移在95%的next-token概率质量上余弦相似度为0.875。

Conclusion: WFT在无需修改模型权重的情况下有效捕获了监督适应的分布效果，为个性化等场景提供了训练免费的轻量级替代方案，兼具接近SFT的性能与极低的计算开销。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [189] [Defending against Model Extraction for GNNs with Model Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11495)
*Yan Wen,Zhenyi Wang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphRP是一种针对图神经网络模型提取攻击的主动防御框架，通过结构感知的门控机制和拓扑原型驱动的模型重编程，在保持良性查询性能的同时显著削弱攻击者的模型估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN防御存在"欧几里得偏差"，即简单地将图像领域的防御策略（如随机噪声）迁移到图数据上，忽略了节点间复杂的拓扑依赖关系，导致严重的效用退化。被动方法如水印无法实时阻止模型窃取。因此需要一种能利用图拓扑结构的主动防御方法。

Method: GraphRP将模型重编程思想应用于安全防御，提出结构感知门控机制，由可学习的拓扑原型驱动。该机制动态调节模型决策边界：对位于训练流形上的良性查询保持保真度，同时沿扰动方向最大化Fisher信息以扰乱对抗查询。

Result: 在硬标签和软标签模型提取攻击上进行了大量实验，结果表明GraphRP显著降低了攻击有效性，同时保持了良性查询的效用。

Conclusion: GraphRP通过利用图结构的重编程方法，在攻击者估计误差下限的可证明保证下，实现了对GNN模型提取攻击的有效主动防御，克服了现有方法的欧几里得偏差问题。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) serve as the backbone for high-stakes applications in Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS). Still, their black-box deployment exposes them to Model Extraction (ME) attacks, in which adversaries steal intellectual property by querying APIs. Existing defenses suffer from a critical ''Euclidean bias'': they transfer image-based strategies (e.g., random noise) to graphs, ignoring the complex topological dependencies between nodes, which often results in severe utility degradation. Passive methods like watermarking also fail to prevent theft in real time. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphRP (Graph Reprogramming Protection), a proactive defense framework that repurposes Model Reprogramming for security. Unlike static perturbations, GraphRP introduces a Structure-Aware Gating Mechanism driven by learnable topological prototypes. This creates a dynamic ''structural firewall'' that selectively modulates the model's decision boundary: it preserves fidelity for benign queries residing on the training manifold, while maximizing the Fisher Information along the perturbation direction for adversarial queries. Under standard assumptions (bounded loss, optimal attacker, and local second-order approximation), we prove a lower bound on the attacker's estimation error that increases with the structural sensitivity of the reprogramming noise. Extensive experiments on both hard-label and soft-label ME attacks demonstrate that GraphRP significantly degrades attack effectiveness while preserving benign utility.

</details>


### [190] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂，用于离线强化学习超参数选择。研究评估了包括基于拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型在内的多种方法，在高维非平稳传感器数据上的预测任务中表现出色，可生成逼真的长时域rollout并恢复超参数敏感性趋势，为离线动力学模型支持真实世界RL部署提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统中部署的关键障碍是超参数选择，尤其在无模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。先前的校准模型方法仅在简单模拟环境中评估，缺乏真实工业场景的验证。作者希望在复杂的真实世界环境中检验这些方法的有效性。

Method: 作者在市政水处理厂这一真实工业环境中，评估了多种校准模型方法，包括使用拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型。他们在高维、非平稳的传感器数据上进行nexting预测任务，并考察了长时域rollout生成、年规模数据集的可扩展性、预训练智能体微调学习率选择支持，以及分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 研究结果表明校准模型能够生成真实的长期rollout，有效恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势。这些模型可扩展到一年长度的数据集，并能支持预训练智能体的微调学习率选择。研究同时揭示了在真实环境中应用这些方法存在的实际挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究为使用离线动力学模型支持真实世界强化学习部署提供了概念验证，证明了校准模型在工业级复杂环境中的可行性，同时指出了未来工作需解决的重要实际挑战。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [191] [FunnelCausalNet: Funnel-aware Joint Conversion-Revenue Uplift for Multi-tier Coupon Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11675)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Guanlin Chen,Min Jiang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FunnelCausalNet，一种将转化二元头与条件订单价值非负头耦合的增量估计器，通过μ_gmv=μ_conv×μ_val的确定性漏斗建模GMV。在RCT假设下，通过理想化均方误差分析识别漏斗组合可降低逐点方差的机制，并结合边际分裂共形CATE汇总、Bonferroni联合审计带及拉格朗日预算分配器。在半合成Criteo-MT7上达到领先基线水平，在工业酒店优惠券RCT数据上7个锚点均取得最佳DeltaROI，同时明确了稀疏基准上的决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优惠券活动旨在同时提升转化率和收入，但GMV遵循从转化到条件订单价值的确定性漏斗，具有零膨胀和重尾分布特征。现有增量估计方法未充分利用这一漏斗结构，且直接回归GMV在零膨胀场景下误差较大，需要一种能耦合转化与订单价值、同时支持预算约束分配的估计框架。

Method: FunnelCausalNet通过μ_gmv=μ_conv×μ_val耦合二元转化头和非负条件价值头。理论上，在RCT、支撑、率差及交叉头协方差控制假设下，通过理想化前导阶MSE比较识别漏斗组合能降低逐点方差的机制（启发式而非神经网络保证）。配对边际分裂共形CATE摘要，通过Bonferroni并集作为审计带；使用RCT锚定估计的拉格朗日预算分配器实现补贴感知的ROI核算。

Result: 在半合成多层级Criteo-MT7上，FunnelCausalNet的AUUC_GMV均值在11个基线中处于领先特征交互基线的种子标准差内；受控消融在测试的零膨胀机制下将GMV效应误差较直接GMV回归降低18-48%。在约490万曝光记录的工业酒店优惠券RCT数据上，7个相关锚点（10%-60%）均取得最佳种子平均DeltaROI。在稀疏二元支出公开基准上，收入导向排名器可主导提升曲线代理指标，定义明确机制边界。

Conclusion: 漏斗组合式增量估计在零膨胀重尾GMV场景下可行且有效，理论分析提供启发式方差降低机制，工业与半合成实验均验证其有效性。但需注意结果的描述性（种子平均一致性）而非独立显著性，且在稀疏支出场景存在明确的适用边界。

Abstract: Coupon campaigns seek to lift both conversion and revenue, but gross merchandise value (GMV) follows a deterministic funnel from conversion to conditional order value and is zero-inflated and heavy-tailed. We propose FunnelCausalNet, an uplift estimator coupling a binary conversion head with a nonnegative conditional-value head through $μ_{\mathrm{gmv}}=μ_{\mathrm{conv}}μ_{\mathrm{val}}$. Under explicit RCT, support, rate-gap, and cross-head covariance-control assumptions, an idealized leading-order MSE comparison identifies a regime in which funnel composition can reduce pointwise variance; this is a heuristic, not a guarantee for the shared-representation neural model. The estimator is paired with marginal split-conformal CATE summaries, combined through a Bonferroni union as audit bands, and a Lagrangian budgeted allocator using RCT-anchored estimates for subsidy-aware ROI accounting. On semi-synthetic multi-tier Criteo-MT7, FunnelCausalNet's mean AUUC_GMV is within one seed standard deviation of the leading feature-interaction baseline among eleven baselines, while a controlled ablation reduces GMV effect error versus direct GMV regression by 18--48% across tested zero-inflation regimes. On de-identified industrial Hotel-Coupon RCT logs with about 4.9 million hold-out exposure records per seed, expected-outcome evaluation sweeps full LP frontiers; FunnelCausalNet has the best seed-averaged mean DeltaROI at all seven correlated anchors from 10% to 60%, which we treat as descriptive frontier consistency rather than independent significance. On sparse binary-spend public benchmarks, revenue-focused rankers can dominate uplift-curve proxies, defining an explicit regime boundary.

</details>


### [192] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向数据稀缺电力市场的电价预测迁移框架，借助Chronos-2时间序列基础模型，通过多源市场信息（MSMI）接口和门控低秩适配器（LoRA）实现跨市场迁移，仅更新约1%的模型参数。在中国四个省级日前现货市场验证，平均MAE/RMSE相比零样本Chronos-2降低6.24%/7.99%，相比传统Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但电价波动大、市场特异性强，现有监督方法高度依赖市场历史数据，在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中难以应用。亟需一种不依赖目标市场标签的跨市场迁移方案。

Method: 首先构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与预清算供需、备用、维护、发电机容量和联络线变量对齐；然后训练源域门控低秩适配器（LoRA），仅更新约1%参数；门控机制根据备用紧张度和运行状态信号缩放冻结的源适配器；采用留一市场法（leave-one-market-out）评估跨市场迁移性能。

Result: 在中国四个省级日前现货市场实验中，所提框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2平均MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%，相比传统Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验显示，学习全局标量或随机门控初始化的增益不可复现，但相对Source-LoRA的额外提升有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入与状态依赖的门控LoRA为数据稀缺电力市场提供了可行的迁移路径，但门控机制带来的增益有限，未来可能需要进一步探索更有效的适配策略。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [193] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键发现：大语言模型的词表大小（vocabulary size）不应作为训练时的固定常量，而应作为部署时期的服务场景函数来优化。作者形式化了全生命周期部署成本模型，并通过在A10G和A100两种GPU上的控制实验证明：推理最优词表随服务批量大小从32k（B=1）到524k（B=64+）变化达16倍；生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中可相差16倍。由于质量在该最优区间内近似不变（BPB差异<2%），词表大小本质上是纯系统优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施中的基础设计决策，但传统上仅凭惯例在训练时固定，缺乏基于部署场景的系统分析。作者旨在证明成本最优词表不是常量，而是服务制度（serving regime）的函数，从而为容量规划提供可操作指导。

Method: 作者将全生命周期部署成本形式化为C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量、B为服务批量大小。通过在两种GPU（A10G和A100）上进行控制实验，覆盖从内存受限到计算受限的不同服务制度，系统测量不同词表大小下的推理成本和训练质量（BPB）。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表随服务批量大小从32k（B=1）大幅移至524k（B=64+），变化达16倍，由V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销驱动；(2) 在1.3-2.3B模型规模下，质量最优词表为65k，确认词表偏好具有规模依赖性；(3) 生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中可相差16倍。质量在最优区间内近似不变（BPB差异<2%）。

Conclusion: 词表大小应作为纯系统优化问题处理，在测量范围内不影响质量。具体指导：设备端部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131-262k。这为生产环境中的容量规划提供了直接可执行的建议。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [194] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，用于降低强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）的计算成本。作者识别出统计上的关键不匹配：未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度不是独立点贡献之和，而是滚动轨迹对上的二阶U统计量。PAIR将短滚动前缀视为顶点、配对梯度项视为边，构建对比图，通过前缀预测器和凸设计来选择正延续概率，并对每条边按其联合包含概率的倒数加权，从而获得设计无偏的估计器。实验表明，在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上，PAIR相比最强逐点分配器提升平均准确率+1.2和+1.4，同时比全组GRPO少生成51%和52%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR中的token分配器基于逐点难度或效用概念分配预算，但作者发现组相对分数梯度本质上是成对（pairwise）的二阶U统计量，而非独立点贡献之和。这种统计上的错配导致现有的逐点分配器在自适应选择端点时产生偏差，因此需要一个感知成对结构的分配方法。

Method: PAIR方法构建对比图：以短滚动前缀为顶点、成对梯度项为边。采用前缀预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本；凸设计在期望后缀token预算约束下选择正延续概率；每个由已完成顶点诱导的边按其记录的对联包含概率倒数进行逆加权。在条件独立on-policy rollouts和未裁剪、未标准化目标下，所得估计器对完整候选对梯度是设计无偏的。

Result: 在计算匹配的RLVR运行中（Qwen3-1.7B/4B），PAIR相比最强逐点分配器平均准确率提升+1.2和+1.4，同时比全组GRPO减少51%和52%的生成token。冻结种群估计器审计确认：未加权的自适应选择有偏，而成对包含校正能在匹配后缀成本下恢复完整对目标。

Conclusion: PAIR通过识别并利用RLVR梯度估计中固有的成对结构，实现了更高效且统计上无偏的token分配。该方法在显著减少计算成本的同时提升模型准确率，证明了"感知成对结构"的自适应分配优于传统的逐点分配策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [195] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对当地区域供热系统某变电站的传输热能数据，结合室外环境温度，测试了多种多元离群值检测方法（包括Z-score基准、马氏距离、PCA、隔离森林和Hotelling T²检验），旨在识别异常工厂运行，最终采用三种有效方法（PCA、隔离森林、Hotelling）的集成方法作为最终方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示异常工厂运行，更广泛的目标是识别减少中央供暖厂燃气消耗和CO2排放的机会。研究还考虑了特定领域情况，如零传输能量时间点不代表离网工厂。

Method: 测试了五种离群值检测方法：Z-score（单变量基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、隔离森林和Hotelling T²检验。考虑了室外环境温度作为附加变量，并与领域专家讨论结果的合理性。

Result: PCA、隔离森林和Hotelling方法提供了相关结果，而其他方法效果不佳。最终采用这三种方法一致同意的离群点作为集成方法结果。

Conclusion: 基于三种有效方法的一致协议采用集成方法作为最终检测方案，可有效识别供热系统中的异常运行，为节能减排提供指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [196] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带背包约束的上下文赌博机（Contextual Bandits with Knapsack）问题的新算法。该问题中，决策者需要为每个到达的客户分配产品，每个产品消耗有限的资源并产生随机回报。作者提出了UCB算法族的自然简单扩展，并结合重新优化技术，实现了O((ln T)³/T)的平均遗憾，显著优于文献中基于重新优化的动态定价问题的O(1/√T)界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题在实际中具有广泛的应用（如动态定价、资源分配等），但现有基于重新优化技术的算法遗憾界限为O(1/√T)，仍有改进空间。作者希望设计更简单的算法并获得更紧的遗憾界限。

Method: 作者提出了UCB（上置信界）算法族的自然且简单的扩展，将线性收益函数的学习与重新优化技术相结合。算法在每个决策步骤利用置信区间来估计未知的线性收益函数，并通过重新优化来动态调整分配策略。

Result: 该算法实现了O((ln T)³/T)的平均遗憾，其中T为时间范围。这一结果显著优于文献中基于重新优化的相关动态定价问题的O(1/√T)界限。

Conclusion: 通过利用重新优化技术，所提出的简单UCB扩展算法在带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中取得了远优于现有文献的遗憾界限，展示了重新优化在提升算法性能方面的强大潜力。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [197] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式引擎（如AI搜索引擎）中内容提供者与平台之间的"引用战争"问题。作者将供应商-平台交互建模为具有部分监测的重复Stackelberg博弈，并提出一种名为VCR（基于可验证内容奖励）的平台-创作者机制，通过奖励具有可核查事实内容的改写来对齐创作者激励与答案可信度，在三个基准上平均超越最强基线12.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎正在重塑网络生态，引用成为注意力、归属和下游价值分配的关键机制。这导致内容提供者倾向于优化以获得模型引用，而平台需要维护答案质量和可信归属，两者之间存在战略性张力，这种张力可能升级为"引用战争"——攻击性GEO会产生降低文档质量并引入无依据主张的改写。

Method: 作者将供应商-平台交互形式化为具有部分监测的重复Stackelberg博弈；通过局部最优响应分析识别引用竞争何时趋近惰性稳态；在此基础上提出VCR机制，不仅惩罚可疑改写，还奖励那些呈现可核查事实内容的改写，从而对齐创作者激励与答案可信度。

Result: 在三个基准上进行实验，VCR一致实现了最大的净防御效用分数，平均超过最强基线12.1个百分点，并在经验等价性标准下产生双赢结果。

Conclusion: VCR机制有效缓解了生成式引擎生态中的引用战争问题，通过奖励可验证内容而非仅惩罚恶意行为，实现了平台防御效用与创作者利益的平衡，为生成式搜索生态的激励机制设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome. Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [198] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，用于检测离线强化学习在ICU治疗决策中的"毒性模仿"故障——即智能体复现有害治疗模式（如临终关怀期间停药）。通过基于脓毒症救治指南的生理扰动，发现标准评估指标（MSE和FQE）无法捕捉这一风险，而CCA审计揭示MedDT在乳酸升高时反而降低血管加压药剂量，违背复苏指南；HCT-RL则保持生理一致性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习评估指标（MSE和FQE）仅衡量行为模仿和Q值拟合，无法检测"毒性模仿"——智能体复制临床数据中有害的治疗模式（如舒适护理过渡期间的停药行为）。这可能导致在安全关键的ICU场景中部署不安全的治疗策略，需要新的评估框架。

Method: 使用MIMIC-III数据库，构建反事实临床审计（CCA）框架：通过基于脓毒症救治指南的生理扰动来压力测试RL智能体。审计两个模型——Medical Decision Transformer（MedDT）和历史因果Transformer（HCT-RL），后者采用因果动作屏蔽、倾向性重要性加权和保守Q学习。

Result: CCA揭示MedDT在乳酸水平升高时矛盾地减少血管加压药剂量，违背复苏指南；而HCT-RL则维持了生理上一致的反应。这表明统计拟合与临床安全性之间存在系统性错位。

Conclusion: 反事实审计应作为医学强化学习评估的必要标准，因为传统统计指标无法保障临床安全性。研究暴露了标准评估方法在检测有害行为模仿方面的根本局限。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [199] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于引导离散扩散的模型无关组合优化框架，用于解决品类组合优化问题。该方法将品类表示为二进制向量，通过学习的反向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并利用期望收益估计来引导局部转移，从而平衡探索与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的品类组合优化依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），虽然计算上易于处理，但对模型误设敏感，且难以捕捉复杂的顾客行为。

Method: 将品类表示为二进制向量，通过引导离散扩散模型进行随机搜索；引入奖励引导机制，利用期望收益估计偏差局部转移，在生成过程中平衡探索与利用，避免显式的组合枚举。

Result: 经验结果显示该方法能持续识别高质量品类，在模型误设下保持鲁棒性，在高维场景中常能恢复近似最优解，同时扩散模型的生成特性还能产出多样化且高性能的品类。

Conclusion: 生成建模可作为数据驱动决策中可扩展且鲁棒的组合优化范式，为品类组合优化提供超越单一确定性解的灵活方案。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [200] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦聚合方法进行了大规模重建性对比研究，在500个单元（5种聚合方法×5个数据集×5种架构×4种条件）的评估矩阵中，通过追踪执行日志、修复或重跑36次运行、识别454个原始成功日志及10个SVHN摘要支持单元，系统性地比较了各方法的预测性能和鲁棒性。Trimmed Mean在清洁条件下表现最佳（宏平均准确率76.02%，平均任务内排名1.70），Krum在符号翻转和高斯攻击下准确率最高。此外，审计发现BadNets指标实际测度的是触发目标标签率（TTLR），并识别出FedPARETO脚手架中预测摘要与聚合权重可能表征不同模型更新的潜在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦聚合方法的鲁棒性比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行来源（provenance），但已有研究往往缺乏对这些因素的联合审视。本文旨在通过重建大规模评估矩阵并审计指标实现和代码脚手架，提供严格、可追溯的联邦聚合方法对比，同时揭示报告中可能存在的指标语义偏差和实现层面的不一致性。

Method: 构建500单元的种子-1评估矩阵（5种聚合方法×5个数据集×5种架构×4种条件：清洁、符号翻转、高斯、BadNets），通过识别成功执行日志（454个原始运行和36个修复/重跑执行）重建实验结果。对10个仅有摘要来源的SVHN单元进行标注。分别计算清洁条件下的宏平均准确率和平均任务内排名；在21对任务（所有方法-条件组合均有原始成功日志）上进行敏感性分析以验证排名稳定性。对BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO脚手架代码进行审计以确证指标语义和潜在偏差。

Result: Trimmed Mean在清洁条件下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均任务内排名（1.70）；Krum在符号翻转和高斯攻击下取得最高记录准确率。在限制为21对具有完整原始日志的任务时，相对排名保持不变。BadNets指标审计表明所有测试输入在目标标签计数前均已触发，因此该指标实际为TTLR而非传统的排除目标的攻击成功率。FedPARETO审计发现预测摘要与聚合权重可能分别对应未损坏模型和损坏更新，存在报告结果与聚合实际输入不一致的潜在途径。

Conclusion: 研究结果是描述性的比较而非统计估计或通用鲁棒性声明。由于矩阵中每个单元仅有单一种子且攻击与配置的完整谱系不完整，结论应局限在记录配置范围内解释。Trimmed Mean和Krum分别在清洁和对抗条件下表现最佳，但任何排名都不应被推广为普适性结论。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>


### [201] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Click2Poly 是一个基于 Florence-2 视觉语言模型的人机协作 AI 助手，作为 QGIS 插件实现，通过用户点击直接编辑建筑物和墙壁矢量图层，加速制图标准下的手动质量控制与修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物和墙壁的精确矢量建图对地理空间应用至关重要，但人工标注成本高。虽然深度学习方法提升了自动提取效果，但为了满足制图标准，仍需人工进行质量控制和复杂情况的修正，这一手动步骤十分耗时。

Method: 扩展 Florence-2 视觉语言模型（VLM），使模型能够响应用户的点击操作，直接编辑建筑或墙壁矢量图层。并实现为 QGIS 插件，在实际生产环境中使用。

Result: 在真实生产环境中，Click2Poly 能够加速建筑和墙壁矢量图层的手动编辑过程。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 与人工交互结合的人机协作方案可以有效提升制图生产中手动编辑环节的效率，Click2Poly 证明了该方向的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [202] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，在最小内积（Min-IP）任务上，核注意力（kernel attention）与全注意力（full attention）之间存在指数级的能力鸿沟。在长度为2的序列上，秩一归一化核注意力可以精确求解；但对于长度为3的序列，任何单个归一化非负核注意力头若想将误差控制在1/2以下，需要 $2^{Ω(m)}$ 维特征，而稠密softmax注意力仅需 $m$ 维即可常数温度解决。该下界在上下文长度增长时趋近精确的 $2^m$ 特征实现。此外，对确定性多层多头草图模型，还证明了与独立答案数量线性、与字母表大小对数相关的转录下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对核注意力与全注意力表达能力的严格理论对比。本文试图揭示：当上下文长度增长并出现竞争候选时，核注意力的压缩特性（将序列压缩为固定维草图）会导致其表达能力呈指数级劣于全注意力，从而为理解线性注意力方法的局限性提供理论基础。

Method: 作者在布尔输入上的最小内积（Min-IP）任务中构造严格的理论分析。首先展示秩一归一化核注意力在序列长度不超过2时精确可解；随后利用下界论证证明，对3-token序列，任何归一化非负核注意力头即使配备任意有限维tokenwise值和任意查询依赖仿射读出，要达到低于1/2的错误率也需要 $2^{Ω(m)}$ 个特征。同时证明稠密softmax注意力仅需 $m$ 维分数和常数温度即可解决。论文还扩展了位置相关token映射和因果最终查询的设置，并针对确定性多层层级草图模型给出转录下界。

Result: 严格证明了核注意力与全注意力在Min-IP任务上的指数级表达能力差距。具体而言：(1) 长度≤2的序列秩一核注意力可精确求解；(2) 3-token序列上核注意力需要 $2^{Ω(m)}$ 特征才能达到低于1/2的错误率，而softmax注意力仅需 $m$ 维；(3) 随着上下文长度增长，下界逼近精确的 $2^m$ 特征实现（匹配上界）；(4) 对确定性草图模型给出转录复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了核注意力因序列压缩导致的指数级表达能力天花板，为“线性注意力无法完全替代softmax注意力”提供了坚实的理论依据。结果启发后续研究需设计更复杂的核注意力变体（如多层、多头或混合机制）以弥补单头核注意力的表达力不足。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [203] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoGrable，一个无需训练图模型即可自动构建图（用于GNN）的准则与方法。其核心洞察是：在最小表格到图抽象中，消息传递GNN（受限于1-WL）只把构造看作行的一种颜色细化划分，因此好的图构造应使划分能区分不同标签的行、且不拆分相同标签的行。AutoGrable将此准则转化为构造流程，通过标签对齐风险（加上占用惩罚）为候选列子集打分，无需物化图也无需训练GNN，可贪心高效搜索，并适用于单表和外键模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格和关系数据库本身不带有图结构，而应用GNN前必须决定哪些实体作为节点、哪些相连、通过何种关系。现有做法依赖人工选择、模式启发式或逐一训练候选图再择优，成本高且缺乏理论依据。作者希望给出一个不依赖训练图模型的简单准则来指导图构造。

Method: 1) 在最小表到图抽象中，每行是一个节点；消息传递GNN（受1-WL约束）只把构造看作行被划分为颜色细化类。2) 判断构造好坏的标准：该划分应分离不同标签的行、且不拆分同标签的行。3) 对关联（incidence）构造，划分由所选列固定，因此构图简化为选列。4) 用标签对齐风险评分候选列子集：块的常数最优预测器的留出风险，加上衡量块占用稀疏度的惩罚项。5) 该分数不物化图也不训练GNN，因此可贪心低成本地搜索子集空间，返回grable，适用于单表和外键模式。

Result: 实验表明：在候选图空间中，该分数能丢弃大部分候选而保留最优者；在受控任务上能恢复生成标签的列，并在真实任务上以固定预测器优于固定、随机和任务感知的构造器；它是唯一能在没有任何图有助于任务时拒绝构建图的方法。

Conclusion: AutoGrable提供了一种无需训练图模型、理论基础清晰且计算高效的自动图表构造方法，能有效选择有用的列/图结构，并能诚实地判定何时不应构建图。这为表格上的GNN应用带来了更可靠、更省算力的前置步骤。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model.
