Method: 作者提出一个差分分析框架：将加载目标技能的运行与无技能或语义匹配技能的参考运行进行对比，从而将失败或成本回归归因到特定技能。该框架在SkillsBench和SWE-Skills-Bench两个基准上实例化，共识别出307个技能引发的失败（125个功能性失败和182个效率回归）。同时构建了SkillTriage工具，这是一个基于分类学的归因工具，能够规范化配对案例、提取差分证据并生成分类报告。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）技能引发的功能性失败很少由明显不相关的技能导致，而是看似相关的技能常常使智能体错误实施或遗漏任务必需的实现要素；（2）技能引发的效率回归不能仅用提示长度来解释；（3）在过度程序（Excessive Procedure）类别中，最大来源是过度验证（67例）和重型实施流程（30例），表明技能常将验证清单和构建配方变成强制性工作。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者提出了关于更安全和更成本感知的技能复用（skill reuse）的研究方向与工具改进建议。

Abstract: Agent skills are the de facto mechanism for extending LLM agents with reusable guidance. A skill can shape the agent's task execution, including planning, tool use, problem-solving, and validation. Prior work reported mixed results of agent skills: some skills improve task success rates, while others have no effect, increase token use and execution time, and even reduce success rates. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of skill-induced agent failures by attributing task failures and cost regressions to specific loaded skills. We introduce a differential analysis framework that attributes a failure or regression to a skill by comparing a target skill-guided run against a no-skill or semantically matched skill reference run that solves the same task, or solves it more cheaply. We instantiate this framework on SkillsBench and SWE-Skills-Bench, yielding 307 skill-induced failures, including 125 functional failures and 182 efficiency regressions. We also build SkillTriage, a taxonomy-guided attribution tool that normalizes paired cases, extracts differential evidence, and produces triage reports. Our major findings include: (1) Skill induced functional failures are rarely caused by obviously irrelevant skills; instead, seemingly relevant skills often make the agent incorrectly implement or omit task-required implementation elements. (2) Skill-induced efficiency regressions are not explained by prompt length alone. (3) The largest sources within Excessive Procedure are excessive verification and heavy implementation pipelines, contributing 67 and 30 cases, respectively. This shows that skills often turn validation checklists and construction recipes into mandatory work. Based on our findings, we propose research topics and tooling improvements for safer and more cost-aware skill reuse.

</details>


### [339] [Policy-as-logic for robust reasoning over rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11905)
*Rahul Nair,Bastian Lipka,Elizabeth Daly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合符号方法，将书面政策表达为形式逻辑，在推理时利用语言模型进行事实抽取来接地谓词，并使用回答集求解器进行推理，使生成式AI系统的响应可解释、可审计、准确且对输入扰动鲁棒。该方法在多数情况下优于policy-as-prompt和policy-as-code方法，并减少了约10倍的token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统在实际应用中（如税务规则、航空公司行李限额）需要遵守书面政策或规则，但现有方法（如直接提示或编码政策）在准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面存在不足。

Method: 将政策表达为形式逻辑；在推理时将事实抽取与推理步骤分离——用语言模型抽取事实以接地谓词，用回答集求解器（answer set solver）进行逻辑推理。

Result: 该混合方法在多数场景下优于政策即提示（policy-as-prompt）和政策即代码（policy-as-code）方法，且token使用量减少了约10倍，同时展现了良好的准确性和对输入扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将结构化推理和符号求解器与生成模型结合，对于涉及客观标准的稳健决策具有重要价值。

Abstract: In many practical applications of generative AI systems, from tax rules to airline baggage allowance, responses to natural language queries must respect written policies or rules. We present a hybrid symbolic approach that expresses policies in formal logic and at inference time exploits the representation power of language models for fact extraction to ground predicates, and an answer set solver for reasoning such that responses are interpretable, auditable, and as we show, accurate and robust under input perturbations. Specifically, we show this separation of extraction and reasoning steps outperforms policy-as-prompt and policy-as-code methods in most cases with ~10x reduction in token usage. The results point to the value of structured reasoning and symbolic solvers in conjunction with generative models to make robust decisions involving objective criteria.

</details>


### [340] [OEIS Open: How many conjectures can language models turn into theorems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11941)
*Tom Adamczewski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了OEIS Open基准，包含492个来自OEIS的开放数学猜想（由Tsoukalas等人用Lean形式化）。使用最小工具集和每尝试50美元预算的LM解决了147个猜想（30%得分）；使用200美元预算时，最佳LM在OEIS Open Lite（100个猜想的子集）上得分为44%。访问arXiv文献或更复杂的智能体循环并未提升性能。结果显示LM能以适度成本自主解决开放研究猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往这些猜想仅由专门的定制智能体尝试过，缺乏一个开放源码、通用且能防止LM作弊的评估框架来衡量通用语言模型解决开放数学猜想的能力。

Method: 基于OEIS中的492个开放数学猜想（已用Lean形式化）构建开源评估基准OEIS Open，并设计OEIS Open Lite（100个猜想的随机子集）用于低成本评估。评估框架开源且安全，可运行任何通用LM，配备最小工具集，设置不同的预算（50美元和200美元/尝试）。

Result: 最小工具集LM以50美元预算解决147个猜想（30%得分）；200美元预算下最佳LM在OEIS Open Lite上得分44%。提供arXiv上476,000篇数学文献访问或使用更复杂的智能体循环均未提升性能。

Conclusion: LM能够以适度成本自主解决开放研究猜想，尽管这些猜想数学意义不确定且此前少受关注。该工作表明开源通用评估框架的可行性和LM在数学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: We construct OEIS Open, a benchmark based on 492 open mathematical conjectures from the OEIS, formalized in Lean by Tsoukalas et al. Whereas these conjectures had previously been attempted only with a bespoke agent, our open-source evaluation code runs any generic language model (LM) against them, and is secure against LM cheating attempts. We find that LMs equipped with a minimal set of tools resolve 147 of these conjectures with a budget of \$50 per attempt, scoring 30% on OEIS Open. OEIS Open Lite is a random subset of 100 conjectures for cheaper evaluation. When evaluated with a budget of \$200 per attempt, the best current LM scores 44% on OEIS Open Lite. Giving LMs access to the mathematics literature via 476,000 papers from arXiv did not increase performance on OEIS Open Lite, and nor did using more sophisticated agent loops. The conjectures covered in this work are of uncertain mathematical significance, and most have likely received little previous attention. Nevertheless, our results show that LMs can resolve open research conjectures autonomously and at modest cost.

</details>


### [341] [ExRole: From Team Trajectories to Executable Roles in Multi-Agent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11949)
*Zhou Liu,Chaoyang Han,Zewei Pan,Zeli Su,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExRole是一个轨迹到角色的框架，将角色视为可执行的控制变量，从prefix-local团队轨迹中学习未来感知的角色原型，并将其解析为可读指令和token对齐的角色标记，可选地通过回合对齐的信用分配路由共享LoRA秩槽位。在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上显著优于单智能体搜索和最强非ExRole基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数多智能体系统将角色视为手写的提示标签，与学习行为和参数更新脱节。作者认为有用的角色应该是可执行的控制变量：能够总结预测未来效用的行为、指导后续交互，并识别负责该行为的可训练能力。

Method: ExRole框架从prefix-local团队轨迹中学习未来感知的角色原型，将轨迹解析为可读指令和token对齐的角色标记，并通过回合对齐的信用分配可选地路由共享的LoRA秩槽位。

Result: 在MuSiQue上比单智能体搜索提升15.0/14.4 EM/F1，在2WikiMultiHopQA上提升13.5/16.1 EM/F1。对最强非ExRole控制，增益分别为11.5/11.6和7.7/9.7点。受控结果一致偏好轨迹诱导的角色条件化，优于无角色、手动、随机和打乱替代方案。

Conclusion: Role-Agent-Turn干预表明，诱导出的角色捕获了超越固定智能体身份或回合位置的可转移行为特化，验证了角色作为可学习控制变量的有效性。

Abstract: Roles provide an interpretable interface for organizing language-model agents, yet most multi-agent systems treat them as hand-written prompt labels disconnected from learned behavior and parameter updates. We argue that a useful role should instead be an executable control variable: it should summarize behavior predictive of future utility, guide subsequent interaction, and identify the trainable capacity responsible for that behavior. We introduce ExRole, a trajectory-to-role framework that learns future-aware role prototypes from prefix-local team traces, resolves them into readable instructions and token-aligned role markers, and optionally routes shared LoRA rank slots with turn-aligned credit. Across MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA, ExRole improves over single-agent search by 15.0/14.4 and 13.5/16.1 EM/F1 points, respectively. Against the strongest non-ExRole controls, the corresponding gains remain 11.5/11.6 and 7.7/9.7 points. Across both benchmarks, the controlled results consistently favor trajectory-induced role conditioning over role-free, manual, random, and shuffled alternatives. Role-Agent-Turn interventions further show that the induced roles capture transferable behavioral specialization beyond fixed agent identities or turn positions.

</details>


### [342] [Retry, Switch, or Abstain? Learning Strategy-Aware Tool-Use Policies via Controlled Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11977)
*Chaoran Chen,Vy Nguyen,Ziji Zhang,Abhinav Gullapalli,Ziyi Wang,Yuxuan Lu,Dakuo Wang,Jing Huang,Zhou Yu,Jin Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，将无故障的工具使用基准转化为受控随机环境，其中情节明确要求重试、切换或停止。研究发现所有模型在工具故障下存在普遍的鲁棒性缺口，并提出两种互补干预：贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）提供结构化运行时恢复上下文，以及课程控制的强化学习。两者结合可在保持无故障性能的同时大幅提升鲁棒性（注入下40.8-45.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的LLM代理通常在工具调用可靠成功的环境中训练和评估，但现实部署中工具可能瞬时、持久或静默地失败。鲁棒恢复需要的不只是重试，还包括切换路径或识别无路可走，而现有研究缺乏对这一问题的系统性控制和评估。

Method: 1) 提出BENCH2ROBUST框架：将无故障基准转换为受控随机环境，通过场景控制的"可解性"使情节明确需要重试、切换或停止。2) 提出两种互补干预：贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）提供结构化的运行时恢复上下文；课程控制的强化学习训练恢复行为。

Result: 在4个模型家族、7个模型和两个多轮基准家族上，工具故障产生近乎普遍的鲁棒性缺口。在保留的Retail任务上，BTM无需重训即可将鲁棒性提升最多16.8个百分点；RL学习到互补的恢复行为，在无推理时BTM的情况下依然有益。两者结合在注入下达到40.8-45.5%，同时保持无故障性能。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的工具使用受益于将环境特定的恢复知识与学习的恢复行为相结合，BENCH2ROBUST为系统研究工具故障下的代理恢复提供了一个标准化框架。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are commonly trained and evaluated in environments where tool calls succeed reliably, yet deployed tools can fail transiently, persistently, or silently. Robust recovery therefore requires more than repeated retries: an agent may need to retry the same path, switch to an alternative, or recognize that no viable path remains. We present BENCH2ROBUST, a framework that converts failure-free tool-use benchmarks into controlled stochastic environments with scenario-controlled solvability, where episodes explicitly require retrying, switching, or stopping after available paths are exhausted. We use BENCH2ROBUST to study two complementary interventions: structured runtime recovery context through Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM), and curriculum-controlled reinforcement learning. Across 7 models from 4 families and two multi-turn benchmark families, tool failures produce a near-universal robustness gap. On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>


### [343] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu,Wei Wang,Shixi Liu,Jixin Min,Yingwei Dai,Zhibin Yin,Yirong Chen,Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于声明级证伪（claim-level falsification）的测试时扩展原则，并实例化为CLR（Claim-Level Reliability Assessment）框架——一种无需训练的推理增强方法，将测试时计算从额外的解采样重新分配到定向验证上，通过在多个LLM和推理基准上提升pass@1和自一致性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全迹评估（whole-trace evaluation）常因常规token稀释信号而掩盖决定性错误；同时，在固定模型能力下生成完全正确的解极其困难。作者希望利用"构造正确解需完美推理路径，而反驳错误声明只需找到一个致命缺陷"这一非对称性，更高效地提升推理可靠性。

Method: CLR将每个推理轨迹压缩为一组决策关键声明（decision-critical claims），从而隔离逻辑锚点；随后聚焦于语义证伪（semantic falsification）——通过针对性地搜索负面证据来反驳不正确声明，压缩高置信错误轨迹的生存空间，并采用非线性可靠性评分抑制错误共识。该方法无需训练。

Result: 在4个LLM和4个推理基准上、在匹配预算条件下，CLR普遍优于pass@1和自一致性（self-consistency）。例如在GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25上，CLR比pass@1提升27.15个百分点，并将自一致性准确率从77.50%提升至82.19%，同时节省37.0%的token。

Conclusion: 声明级证伪是一种有效的测试时扩展原则。相比盲目增加采样，将计算预算投向针对性的声明验证能更高效地提升推理性能，且无需训练，具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [344] [CTBench: Evaluating Troubleshooting Capabilities of AI Agents in Realistic Telecom Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12002)
*Xingyu Yan,Tingting Dai,Antonio De Domenico,Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Changchang Li,Bowen Liu,Kun Jiang,Mengjie Zhang,Dingcheng Shan,Jing-Cheng Pang,Chenwei Wu,Sijie Wu,Lianying Chao,Haoran Cai,Jiantao Ye,Xubin Li,Simon Mark Lucas,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CTBench，一个用于评估电信网络运维智能体（Agent）能力的公开基准，聚焦于根因分析和路径恢复两类任务，并通过专家构建的标注数据和基于证据的评估指标，发现现有最先进智能体在根因分析上表现不佳，且即使最终答案正确也常缺乏证据支撑的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估无法真实模拟网络特征，也未能在部分可观测的电信环境下（涉及多厂商、多设备、多协议和多接口）对智能体进行准确评估，因此缺乏一个能够检验智能体是否像称职电信排障工程师一样工作的基准。

Method: CTBench由专家构建任务，涵盖根因分析与路径恢复，并标注丰富任务元数据（包括证据步骤）。评估采用专家依据的指标，同时考核最终答案与诊断证据两个维度。

Result: 实验表明，最先进智能体在路径恢复任务中能较好识别端点，但在根因分析中普遍表现不佳，尤其在接口状态、链路层、服务管理等运维故障上遇到困难。即使智能体给出看似合理或正确的最终答案，也常常无法提供基于证据的诊断。此外，路径恢复更耗费资源，但更大的资源投入并不必然带来更好的诊断效果。

Conclusion: CTBench揭示了当前智能体在电信运维中的关键短板——证据驱动的诊断能力不足，表明未来需要开发能够提供可验证、证据充分诊断的智能体，而不仅仅是追求最终答案的正确性。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly considered for automating network operations and maintenance, where engineers must diagnose network faults, optimize configurations to enhance services, and reduce operational costs while acting under strict constraints. However, existing evaluations fail to accurately model real network characteristics or assess agents under partially observable telecom environments with diverse vendors, devices, protocols, and interfaces. In this paper, we introduce CTBench, a public benchmark for assessing whether an agent behaves like a competent telecom troubleshooting engineer. CTBench focuses on root cause analysis and path restoration. Each task is constructed by experts and annotated with rich task metadata, including golden evidence steps. CTBench uses expert-grounded metrics that evaluate both final answers and the diagnostic evidence. Experiments with representative harness-model combinations show that state-of-the-art agents perform very well at identifying endpoints in path-restoration tasks but, more generally, underperform in root cause analysis. In particular, agents struggle with interface state, link-layer, service-management, and other operational faults. Most importantly, even when agents produce plausible or correct final answers, they often fail to provide the evidence-grounded diagnoses required in operational practice. Our results further show that path restoration is generally more resource expensive, yet larger resource usage does not necessarily translate into better diagnosis.

</details>


### [345] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mechanist，一个将AI作为科学仪器来自动化发现AI智能机制的代理系统。该系统构建了约13,000篇可解释性论文的知识图谱，整合了涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇论文数据库，并精选了32种机制分析方法。Mechanist能生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验，展示了从发现模型行为到解释和操控AI模型的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在多个领域取得显著成功，但其能力背后的机制和潜在风险仍未得到充分理解。随着AI开发速度加快且日益自动化，机制探索仍主要依赖人工，导致"模型能做到什么"与"我们理解和控制它们的能力"之间的差距不断扩大。

Method: 构建Mechanist代理系统：1) 构建约13,000篇可解释性论文的知识图谱；2) 整合涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇论文的多学科数据库；3) 精选32种用于机制分析、因果干预和验证的基础方法库。系统利用AI作为科学仪器，自主执行从假设生成到实验验证的完整机制发现流程。

Result: 与Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统相比，Mechanist能生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验。具体成果包括：1) 发现跨模态的安全风险传递问题；2) 发展出信念的机制理论，揭示模型如何表示世界知识、形成信念、推断他人信念及这些机制在预训练中的涌现过程；3) 将这些机制洞察转化为实际干预，改进模型性能和引导科学基础模型生成具有指定属性的DNA序列。

Conclusion: Mechanist证明了自主机制发现的可行性，展示了从发现模型行为到解释模型再到控制模型的完整闭环。该系统弥合了AI能力发展与人类理解和控制之间的差距，为AI安全与可解释性研究提供了自动化工具。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [346] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构化评分标准（GSR），将评分标准编译为类型化评估图，通过命名端口组合标准节点、操作符和输出映射（Readout），并拒绝格式错误或不兼容的图。在GPT-OSS-120B上，点式评分在四个数据集上相比Prometheus风格提高0.62–6.75个百分点的精确评分一致率，成对评估在两个偏好基准上取得数值最高的端到端准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的评估器将标准仅作为提示上下文或扁平标准处理，明确了"评判什么"但将标准组合方式隐式化，即使自然语言规则已明确说明组合逻辑，也未被显式建模。

Method: GSR将评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评估图：标准节点产生判断；变换、约简和门控操作符通过命名端口组合判断；任务特定的输出映射（Readout）将唯一的汇节点转换为分数或偏好。编译阶段拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图。点式评估先对各维度单独评判再图聚合；成对评估复用同一图，每个候选在每个标准下进行一次判断。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B上，GSR在四个点式数据集上相比Prometheus风格评分提高0.62–6.75个百分点的精确评分一致率，并在原生平局和弃权策略下于两个偏好基准上取得数值最高的端到端成对准确率。

Conclusion: 显式建模评分标准组合结构（而非扁平提示）能提升评估准确性与一致性，且该结构适用于点式与成对评估两类任务。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [347] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GUIDE，一个基于治理的多智能体框架，用于处理企业准则文档的解析、提取、验证和产物生成全流程。在120份真实文档上达到96%成功率，将处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业准则文档异构且多模态，现有LLM/VLM系统存在幻觉、表格结构退化、缺乏超越提取的治理工作流等问题，导致企业需人工处理每份文档2-3天，效率低下。

Method: GUIDE构建于共享版本化规则库之上，采用模式验证的智能体间契约和端到端溯源追踪。六个专业智能体分别负责解析、VLM驱动提取、一致性检查、评估、人类参与（HITL）升级和个性化产物合成。

Result: 在120份真实企业准则文档上，GUIDE实现96%文档成功率，提取3,896条规则，其中71.4%自动获批，生成812个可部署产物，单份文档处理时间降至40-125分钟。

Conclusion: GUIDE证明了治理型多智能体框架能够有效解决企业文档处理中幻觉、表结构退化和缺乏工作流治理的问题，显著提升效率并支持部署级产物生成。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [348] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"大语言模型在不同推理条件下排名稳定"的传统假设，通过改变token生成预算（64到4096个token）在三个推理基准上评估四个模型（56,476次推理），发现：3-19%的条目呈现非单调行为（更多预算反而降低准确率）；模型排名在所有基准上随预算变化而逆转；Oracle分析显示模型互补性最高可达+27.8个百分点；预算感知路由器可捕获14.1%的Oracle差距。作者主张采用预算条件化的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的大语言模型评估假设模型排名在不同推理条件下是稳定的，但这一假设可能不成立。作者想探究token生成预算（最大可生成token数）的变化如何影响模型表现和排名，从而挑战现有评估范式的根本假设。

Method: 在七个预算水平（64-4096 tokens）上评估四个LLM模型，覆盖三个推理基准共56,476次推断。通过控制截断效应来识别非单调行为；使用McNemar检验验证排名逆转的统计显著性；进行Oracle分析测量模型互补性；设计预算感知路由器来评估预算特征的实用价值。

Result: (i) 3-19%条目呈非单调行为（预算增加但准确率下降），且该现象为模型特有（跨模型重叠仅6-14%）；(ii) 所有基准上模型排名随预算逆转（p<0.01，McNemar检验）；(iii) Oracle分析显示模型互补性最高可达+27.8pp，在预算受限时最显著；(iv) 预算感知路由器跨域捕获14.1%的Oracle差距；预算特征在域内有效（+1.6至+5.7pp）但具有域特异性，损害跨域迁移（-1.2pp）。

Conclusion: 现有的静态评估协议不足以反映模型在不同推理条件下的真实表现，需要采用预算条件化的评估协议。模型排名、互补性和路由决策都强烈依赖于token预算，这要求评估实践必须将推理预算作为关键变量纳入考量。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [349] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了蛋白质结构预测模型在有限外部oracle预算下的多种引导方法，包括FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N以及新扩展使用的O3方法。实验发现没有任何单一方法在所有预算和oracle组合下绝对占优：O3在低预算下最有效，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时表现更好，最终为实践者提供了基于预算的可用性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构预测的基础模型在某些目标上仍不可靠，需要外部oracle来标记和纠正失败，但生物oracle成本高昂，oracle预算成为关键约束。现有引导方法在预算分配上各不相同，但缺乏系统比较来指导方法选择，亟需建立首个预算感知引导的实用参考基准。

Method: 在蛋白质结构预测模型上扩展应用O3（Optimisation Over Outputs）方法，系统基准测试四种引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N、O3），在两种蛋白质靶标（钙调蛋白1CLL和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶9EEH）上，跨不同oracle预算和多种oracle进行公平对比评估。

Result: 在两种蛋白质靶标上的评估显示，没有单一方法在所有预算和oracle组合下一致占优。O3在低oracle预算下表现最佳，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时展现出更优性能。研究首次提供了oracle预算感知引导的实际参考基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为面临真实世界oracle预算约束的实践者提供了可操作的建议：低预算场景推荐O3，预算充足时FK-steering和DPO更具优势。这是首个系统性比较oracle预算感知引导方法的研究，填补了该领域的方法选择指导空白。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [350] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代理化（agentic）工作流，用于大规模现代化遗留 Fortran 代码。通过三个提示词专门化的 AI 代理角色、版本控制的规格说明和人工门槛控制，成功将 GAMESS 量子化学软件包中 56,448 行、225 个子程序的二电子积分例程从 Fortran 77 转换为 Fortran 2008，并以位级精确重现测试能量作为合并标准，所有测试均通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留 Fortran 代码的现代化改造面临规模问题：单个转换本身常规，但代码库巨大，且计算科学界大量此类工作未能完成。作者希望探索 AI 代理能在多大程度上承担这类大规模代码现代化任务。

Method: 提出一种代理化工作流：(1) 三个提示词专门化的代理角色（Claude Code）在隔离的 worktrees 中工作；(2) 代理自行撰写和修订版本控制的规格说明；(3) 人类仅保留少量门槛控制点；(4) 利用领域固有的精确验证预言机（位级能量复现）作为安全边界；以位级精确重现 GAMESS 标准测试输出作为合并标准。

Result: 成功将 12 个源文件（56,448 行、225 个子程序）从固定格式 Fortran 77 转换为自由格式 Fortran 2008。全部通过 51 项测试验证（49 项标准 GAMESS 测试和 2 项额外计算），612 次测试运行中化学相关的差异为零，所有文件也通过 Jenkins CI 测试。工作跨越四代 Claude 模型。

Conclusion: 该工作流证明了 AI 代理能够在生产规模上安全地完成遗留代码现代化改造，其安全边界恰好由领域固有的验证预言机界定。12 位小数的位级精确性能作为合并标准，使得代理可以在人工少量监督下可靠地完成任务。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [351] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VAKRA基准测试，包含超过8000个可执行API和62个领域，用于评估企业级代理在结构化API和文档集合上的推理能力。测试涵盖三种难度递增的任务设置。研究发现即使最好的模型在单跳任务上仅达70.4%，组合API任务降至50-51%，推理深度增加时性能下降超50%，策略约束问题严重失败（不可回答查询低至2.4%）。失败集中在语言介导的推理而非工具调用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将企业代理的API推理和知识检索能力分开评估，无法真实反映代理在真实企业环境中需要同时处理结构化API和文档集合的复杂情况。

Method: 构建VAKRA基准，包含8000+可执行API覆盖62个领域，设计三种难度递增的任务设置：多样化API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、多源推理加上自然语言工具使用策略约束。通过重新执行预测的工具调用与实时API验证正确性，并支持多种有效路径。使用固定ReAct框架隔离模型能力与代理架构。

Result: 最好的模型在单跳端点风格任务上仅达70.4%，组合API任务降至50-51%；随着推理深度增加性能下降超50%；策略约束问题表现严重失败，不可回答查询准确率低至2.4%。痕迹分析显示失败集中在实体消歧、跨源定性等语言介导推理，而非工具调用的机制层面。

Conclusion: 企业级代理的核心瓶颈在于语言介导的推理能力（如实体消歧和跨源推理），而非工具调用机制本身。这为未来代理模型改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [352] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化框架，利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM），从系统描述中自动构建动态主逻辑（DML）模型并表示为知识图谱（KG-DML），并将其应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，验证了该方法在复杂大型系统中的可行性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML为系统行为提供了分层表示框架，但其传统构建方式依赖专家对技术文档的解读，难以扩展到复杂系统。因此需要一种自动化方法来提高DML构建的可扩展性和效率。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM）作为核心工具，沿DML层级进行定向检索，在保持功能依赖关系和显式逻辑关系的前提下自动构建KG-DML。同时提出多层级验证方法，评估各层的精度和召回率、逻辑门一致性以及整体结构完整性。

Result: 将框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，在多次重复运行中实现了一致的模型重建，验证了自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转化为可执行的功能模型，用于诊断和可靠性分析。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转化为可执行的功能模型，支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪，为复杂系统的智能建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [353] [Predictive Allostatic Organization in Recurrent and Spiking Agents Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11506)
*Frederick Hayes*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本研究基于Barrett和Miller的预测性、压缩性、功能性组织和稳态约束的分类框架，测试循环脉冲智能体在能量受限觅食任务中是否发展出相应的计算特性。结果表明智能体发展出分布式的、预测性的、能量敏感的、行动相关的内部状态组织，能在部分可观测条件下实现自适应行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测条件下的自适应行为依赖于超越当前观测信息的内部组织。作者希望借鉴Barrett和Miller关于分类的理论框架（预测性、压缩性、功能性组织和稳态约束），检验循环和脉冲智能体是否发展出具有这些计算特性的内部状态，从而理解智能体如何通过内部表征在不确定环境中实现有效决策。

Method: 智能体在能量受限的觅食任务中运行，需要资源获取、威胁规避、接触依赖消耗和内部能量变量调节。实验包括：1) 冻结基准测试中对比学习智能体与随机/启发式基线；2) 分析早期内部动力学对未来成功表现的预测能力；3) PCA降维验证行为相关信息保留；4) 特征族消融实验（trace、策略头、内部动力学、观测和稳态变量）；5) 评估时对时间状态、感官信息、操作条件和稳态机制的扰动测试；6) 种子平衡事件探针测试。

Result: 学习智能体优于随机和启发式基线；trace增强的循环策略整体最强，脉冲变体显示压力特定差异。早期内部动力学对未来全-安全-高效成功预测达到0.802的ROC-AUC。预测信号分布在多种特征族中，低能量状态在移除能量相关特征后仍可强解码。扰动实验改变行为和/或内部预测。事件探针显示对未来事件的较弱但可测量的信息，同时低能量解码强。

Conclusion: 内部状态组织可被解读为预测性稳态组织的计算类似物：分布式控制体系具有预测性、能量敏感性、行动相关性和部分因果参与性。但作者不声称生物学验证或离散符号类别。

Abstract: Adaptive behavior under partial observability depends on internal organization that carries information beyond the current observation. Drawing on Barrett and Miller's account of categorization as predictive, compressive, functionally organized, and allostatically constrained, we test whether recurrent and spiking agents develop internal states with corresponding computational properties. Agents operate in an energy-constrained foraging task requiring resource acquisition, threat avoidance, contact-dependent consumption, and regulation of an internal energy variable. In a frozen benchmark, learned agents outperform random and heuristic baselines; the trace-augmented recurrent policy is strongest overall, while spiking variants show stress-specific differences. Early internal dynamics predict later full-safe-efficient success above permutation baseline, reaching a maximum ROC-AUC of 0.802. Reduced PCA subspaces retain behaviorally relevant information. Feature-family controls show that predictive signal is distributed across trace, policy-head, internal-dynamics, observation, and allostatic variables, and low-energy state remains strongly decodable after explicit energy-related features are removed. Evaluation-time perturbations to temporal state, sensory information, operating conditions, and allostatic mechanisms alter behavior and/or internal prediction. Seed-balanced event probes show weaker but measurable information about future contact, successful consumption, and threat events, alongside strong low-energy decoding. We interpret this pattern as a computational analogue of predictive allostatic organization: distributed control regimes that are predictive, energy-sensitive, action-relevant, and partly causally involved, without claiming biological validation or discrete symbolic categories.

</details>


### [354] [Lapis: Laplacian Spiking Attention via First-Spike Timing and Membrane Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11865)
*Kaiwen Tang,Jiaqi Zheng,Zixuan Zhu,Yiqun Wang,Zhanglu Yan,Weng-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出Lapis，一种基于L1距离和Laplacian核函数的脉冲注意力机制，利用时间到首脉冲编码中查询和键的首脉冲延迟向量距离进行令牌对评分，完全消除查询-键通道间的乘法运算，在CIFAR-10上达到96.56%准确率，在ImageNet-1K上将注意力路径算术能耗降低14.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲视觉Transformer的注意力机制仍继承密集网络的点积评分或使用离散算子替代，但脉冲时间作为脉冲网络的原生变量并未直接用于定义令牌间关系，缺乏利用脉冲时序信息的注意力机制。

Method: 在时间到首脉冲编码下，用查询和键的首脉冲延迟向量之间的L1距离对令牌对评分，通过Laplacian核将距离映射为亲和度。核的指数衰减与LIF膜脉冲响应匹配，行归一化在2的幂次舍入下退化为位移操作，评分仅需减法、绝对值和累加。

Result: CIFAR-10上达到96.56% top-1准确率，仅比点积评分低0.53个百分点；ImageNet-1K上注意力路径算术能耗相对密集点积注意力降低14.5倍；部署的6位模型达到83.25% top-1准确率，每张图像算术能耗3.28mJ。

Conclusion: Lapis证明了利用脉冲时间作为原生变量设计注意力机制是可行且高效的，可以在几乎不损失精度的前提下大幅降低算术能耗，为脉冲视觉Transformer提供了更自然的注意力实现方式。

Abstract: Self-attention has become central to spiking vision transformers, yet its query-key scoring is still largely inherited from dense networks. Existing spiking variants either simplify dot product scoring or replace it with discrete operators, but spike timing, the native variable of a spiking network, does not directly define how tokens are related. We propose Lapis, a spiking attention mechanism that scores each token pair by the L1 distance between its query and key first-spike latency vectors under time-to-first-spike coding, and maps this distance to an affinity through a Laplacian kernel. The kernel's exponential decay matches the impulse response of a leaky integrate-and-fire membrane, so the accumulated latency difference determines the decay of a membrane trace, while row normalization reduces to a bit shift under power-of-two rounding. Scoring therefore needs only subtraction, absolute value, and accumulation, and removes all multiplication between query and key channels. Under a matched backbone and training schedule, Lapis reaches 96.56% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, within 0.53 points of dot-product scoring. On ImageNet-1K, it reduces the estimated arithmetic energy of the attention path by 14.5x relative to dense dot-product attention. The deployed 6-bit model attains 83.25% top-1 accuracy at an estimated arithmetic energy of 3.28mJ per image.

</details>
